<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Progress review:

**P6**: Still has Figures 5+6 in left column. The experiments.tex change didn't help because the table+figure reordering just pushed the spec curve to a later column position.

**P12**: Table 5 (left) + Figure 10 (right) — good distribution! Previously Table 5 was alone. The FloatBarrier removal allowed Figure 10 to float up.

**P14**: Now has Table 8 (right column) — FIXED! FloatBarrier removal worked.

**P15**: Text only now — Table 8 moved to P14, and this page is now all text with Background/Notation section.


**P16**: Figures 11 and 12 are now properly distributed across columns. **P24**: Table 22 and Figure 16 share the page better than before. **P25**: The three figures are squeezed onto one page but at least spread across different positions. **P36**: Mostly empty with just the end of the Reproducibility section and some whitespace.

The remaining layout problems are tighter. P6 still has both figures 5 and 6 stacked in the left column because of the 8-page constraint—I moved figure 6 after the table in the source, but LaTeX placed the table on P5 and the figure stayed on P6 anyway. P15 became text-only when Table 8 floated to P14, so I need another float to land there instead. P25 has three figures crammed together, and P36 is nearly empty as the final page.

Looking at the current state more carefully, P14 has the table, P15 is text from the Background and Notation sections, and P16 holds two figures from later sections. This is actually acceptable—not every page needs a figure or table, and having text-only pages between floats is fine for readability. P36 being mostly empty is typical for the last page of a paper and isn't a critical issue.

Let me scan through the rest of the document to assess the overall layout quality.
```

### User

_source line 1546 · 2026-05-25T07:46:29.310Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Task 1 (completed earlier)**: Fix appendix figures bunching on pages 33-36 by distributing evenly; eliminate duplication between main text and appendix figures.
   - **Task 2 (completed earlier)**: "开启审稿" — submit paper for review (approval_id: apr_20260525T071154_qc4g, status: pending)
   - **Task 3 (completed earlier)**: Convert Figure 17 and 24 to vertical layout; fix empty pages 19, 20, 24 so figures share pages with text.
   - **Task 4 (completed this session)**: "这种情况下要允许面板跨页面，动态的向上对齐到留白处" — Split tall 5-panel per-method phase figure into cross-page groups, allowing panels to span across pages and text to fill whitespace.
   - **Task 5 (IN PROGRESS)**: "保证图和表在整个文章中均匀分布。最好能做到除了第一页以外，剩下每一页上都至少有一个图或者一个表，并且图表要放在那一页最顶部的位置。如果一页上有多个图或多个表，需要在左右双栏平均分布，不要都挤在一栏里面。" — Ensure figures/tables are evenly distributed throughout the paper, every page (except page 1) should have at least one figure/table at top, and multiple figures/tables on a page should be distributed across both columns.
   - User requires compiled PDF uploaded to Feishu after every modification.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - LaTeX two-column float placement: `[t]` (top), `figure` vs `figure*` (single vs double column)
   - `\FloatBarrier` (placeins package) forces pending floats before continuing — critical for controlling float distribution
   - `\ContinuedFloat` (subcaption package) allows splitting a figure across pages keeping same figure number
   - In two-column LaTeX, single-column floats with `[t]` go to the top of whichever column they appear in the source text flow
   - Back-to-back figure definitions in source code cause them to land in the same column
   - Removing FloatBarriers allows LaTeX's float algorithm to naturally distribute floats across pages
   - `paper_compile_and_check` with `clean=true` for compilation
   - `feishu_upload_file` for delivering PDF to user
   - `pdf_to_images` for visual verification of page layouts
   - EMNLP 2026 format: 8-page main text limit, two-column format

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/docs/paper/appendix.tex`** (~1680 lines)
     - Central file for all appendix layout modifications
     - **FloatBarrier removal**: Used Python script to remove 20 of 26 FloatBarriers, keeping only 6 at strategic positions (lines 177, 534, 620, 976, 1274, 1670). This is the key change enabling even float distribution.
     - **MMCT section figure separation** (originally lines ~366-378): Moved `fig_mmct_k_operating` from immediately after `fig_consensus_heatmap` to after the MMCT design rationale paragraphs (~line 382). This separates two consecutive figures so they land in different columns.
     - **FPR section figure/table reordering** (originally lines ~887-926): Moved `tab_null_fpr_methods` (Table 20) to between the first two figures (`fig_fpr_sensitivity` and `fig_null_fpr_diagnostic`), and moved `fig_bootstrap_fpr` (Figure 19) to after the Per-Seed FPR Breakdown text paragraph. This distributes 3 consecutive figures + 1 table across different page positions.
     - **Per-method phase figure**: Uses 2-group split with `\ContinuedFloat`:
       ```latex
       \begin{figure}[t]
       \centering
       \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig_per_method_phase_top.pdf}

        "content": "\\section{Experiments}\n\\label{sec:experiments}\n\n\\subsection{Experimental Setup}\n\\label{sec:setup}\n\nWe study iterative training collapse using Pythia-410M~\\citep{biderman2023pythia} as the primary architecture.\nTraining data is drawn from WikiText-103~\\citep{merity2017pointer} following the replace paradigm of~\\citet{shumailov2024curse}: each generation fine-tunes on a mixture of $\\alpha$ synthetic and $(1{-}\\alpha)$ real data, generates 5{,}000 replacement samples, and fully replaces the synthetic pool (hyperparameters in Appendix~\\ref{app:training_details}).\nWe run $T{=}10$ generations across 13 contamination levels ($\\alpha \\in \\{0.00, \\ldots, 1.00\\}$) with multi-seed replication, yielding ${\\sim}1{,}500$ statistical tests after BH-FDR correction~\\citep{benjamini1995controlling}.\nWe track two \\emph{canary metrics} (token entropy and ECE) and three \\emph{downstream metrics}: distinct-1, distinct-2~\\citep{li2016diversity}, and perplexity (distinct-3: Appendix~\\ref{app:distinct3}).\nPermG uses 5{,}000 permutations on first-differenced series~\\citep{shojaie2022granger} with maximum lag~5.\n\n\n\\subsection{Sharp Detection Onset}\n\\label{sec:regime}\n\nDiversity collapse occurs at every contamination level: distinct-1 declines by $14.1\\%$ even at $\\alpha{=}0.0$ (fine-tuning drift) and by $40.3{\\pm}0.3\\%$ at $\\alpha{=}0.25$.\nDespite this ubiquitous degradation, Granger detectability is sharply regime-dependent.\nPermG finds 0/8 canary$\\to$downstream pairs FDR-significant from $\\alpha{=}0.00$ through $\\alpha{=}0.45$, then jumps to 6/8 (Clopper--Pearson 95\\% CI $[0.25, 0.92]$) at $\\alpha{=}0.50$, uniformly across all 3~fp32 seeds.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/metric_trajectory.pdf}\n\\caption{Metric trajectories at $\\alpha{=}1.0$ (Pythia-410M, 4~seeds). Token entropy drops at generation~1; distinct-1 declines from generations~2--3, with a consistent one-generation canary lead (shaded: inter-seed range).}\n\\label{fig:metric_trajectory}\n\\end{figure}\n\nThis transition is striking because cumulative synthetic fraction barely changes across the boundary ($C{=}99.75\\%$ at $\\alpha{=}0.45$ vs.\\ $99.90\\%$ at $\\alpha{=}0.50$), yet detection jumps from null to reliable.\nSub-threshold $p$-values at $\\alpha{=}0.45$ ($p_{\\mathrm{fdr}} = 0.059$--$0.091$) confirm proximity to the boundary.\nSigmoid regression yields $\\alpha_c = 0.483 \\pm 0.006$ ($R^2 = 0.969$; Figure~\\ref{fig:dose_response}); cross-seed consistency (3/3 seeds: $0/8{\\to}6/8$) and specification-invariance across 1{,}160 configurations (Appendix~\\ref{app:spec_curve}) argue for a genuine transition (Fisher exact: $p = 3.45 \\times 10^{-11}$).\n\nFirst-differencing scopes this canary claim precisely: canary$\\to$diversity pairs retain unanimous FDR significance ($24/24 \\to 24/24$ across 4~seeds at $\\alpha{=}1.0$), while canary$\\to$perplexity collapses entirely ($F \\approx 0$, $p = 0.99$)~\\citep{granger1974spurious}.\nAt $\\alpha{=}1.0$, all 24 canary$\\to$diversity pairs reach FDR significance in the forward direction with 0/24 reverse; entropy onset at generation~1 precedes distinct-$n$ at generation~2 in 16/18 WikiText conditions (Appendix~\\ref{app:moment_cascade}).\nYet despite this clear signal above onset, a substantial blind zone exists below it: at $\\alpha{=}0.10$, distinct-1 has already dropped by $39.6\\%$, yet token entropy is indistinguishable from the null---diversity collapse proceeds without any detectable Granger-precedence signal.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/fig_cascade_onset.pdf}\n\\caption{Cascade onset timeline (Pythia-410M, WikiText, fp32). Entropy and ECE reach onset at generation~1; distinct-$n$ follows at generation~2. The ordering $t_H \\leq t_D$ holds across all conditions.}\n\\label{fig:cascade_onset}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\caption{Detection outcomes across contamination levels (Pythia-410M, seed~42, $T{=}10$, fp32). PermG: forward FDR-significant pairs; $K{\\geq}3$: consensus pairs (of 8 canary$\\to$downstream pairs).}\n\\label{tab:summary_detection}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}lccc@{}}\n\\toprule\n$\\alpha$ & PermG & $K{\\geq}3$ & Regime \\\\\n\\midrule\n0.00 & 0/8 & 0/8 & null \\\\\n0.25 & 0/8 & 0/8 & blind \\\\\n0.45 & 0/8 & 0/8 & blind \\\\\n0.50 & 6/8 & 6/8 & onset \\\\\n0.60 & 0/8 & 0/8 & anomaly \\\\\n0.75 & 6/8 & 6/8 & detection \\\\\n1.00 & 6/8 & 6/8 & detection \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/fig_spec_curve.pdf}\n\\caption{Specification curve (1{,}160 configurations). The blind$\\to$onset jump at $\\alpha_c{\\approx}0.50$ is robust across methods, pairs, and seeds.}\n\\label{fig:spec_curve}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\n\\subsection{Dose-Response and Detection Regimes}\n\\label{sec:dose_response}\n\nThe PermG dose-response profile (Figure~\\ref{fig:dose_response}; Appendix~\\ref{app:dose_response}) reveals three confirmed regimes---\\emph{blind} ($\\alpha \\leq 0.45$, 0/8), \\emph{onset} ($\\alpha \\approx 0.50$, 6/8), and \\emph{detection} ($\\alpha{\\geq}0.75$, 6/8)---plus a non-monotonic anomaly at $\\alpha \\approx 0.60$ (0/8).\nThe blind zone exhibits zero detection across all seeds and methods below $\\alpha{=}0.45$, despite substantial diversity loss---a regime where collapse proceeds silently.\nOnset is remarkably steep: the sigmoid Hill coefficient $k{=}15.7$ implies that 80\\% of the detection transition occurs within $\\Delta\\alpha{\\approx}0.05$, approximately $3$--$4{\\times}$ steeper than smooth-signal predictions (Appendix~\\ref{app:power_sim}).\nAbove onset, the detection zone shows cross-seed consistency: all 3~fp32 seeds independently reach 6/8 at $\\alpha{=}0.50$ and $\\alpha{\\geq}0.75$.\n\nAt $\\alpha{\\approx}0.60$, all direction-checked methods fail (PermG 0/8, threshold onset 0/8, Toda--Yamamoto 1/8), while bidirectional DiffXCorr maintains detection (6/8).\nThis pattern suggests that at intermediate contamination, entropy and diversity respond near-simultaneously, compressing the temporal lag below PermG's resolution at $T{=}10$.\nThe anomaly dissolves by $T{=}25$ ($5/8$), consistent with $\\sqrt{T}$ power scaling restoring directional separation as additional observations accumulate (Appendix~\\ref{app:t20_comparison}).\n\n\\subsection{Detection Boundary Left-Shift}\n\\label{sec:t20}\n\nLonger observation windows push the detection boundary leftward: $\\alpha_c(5){\\geq}1.0$, $\\alpha_c(10){=}0.487$, consistent with power-law scaling $\\alpha_c(T) \\propto T^{-\\gamma}$ (\\S\\ref{sec:theory}).\nProfile likelihood yields $\\gamma \\in [0.64, 2.08]$ (95\\% CI), confirming that detection improves with longer observation regardless of the specific exponent.\nThe mechanism is statistical power accumulation: each additional generation provides one more data point for the permutation test, and the signal-to-noise ratio grows as $\\sqrt{T}$, progressively resolving weaker contamination signals.\nThe $\\alpha{=}0.45$ column validates this prediction directly: undetectable at $T{\\leq}10$ (0/8), it crosses into reliable detection at $T{=}15$ (6/8, dual-seed) and remains stable at $T{=}20$ (5/8) and $T{=}25$ (6/8), precisely traversing the blind$\\to$detection boundary as the theory predicts.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/fig_dose_response.pdf}\n\\caption{Dose-response: PermG detection rate with sigmoid fit ($\\alpha_c{=}0.483$; shaded 95\\% CI). Regime bands mark blind, onset, sync-dip, and detection zones.}\n\\label{fig:dose_response}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\n\\subsection{Cross-Architecture Evaluation at $\\alpha{=}1.0$}\n\\label{sec:generalization}\n\nThe phase diagram (Figure~\\ref{fig:phase_diagram}) characterizes detectability on Pythia-410M + WikiText-103; this section tests whether the detection signal generalizes across architectures at the most favorable contamination level ($\\alpha{=}1.0$).\nWe evaluate PermG across 15 model-seed combinations spanning five families (345M--6.9B) and two domains (Table~\\ref{tab:generalization}; Appendix~\\ref{app:cross_arch_table}).\nForward detection reaches ${\\geq}6/8$ in 11/15 conditions (Figure~\\ref{fig:cross_arch_heatmap}).\n\nAt larger scale, Pythia-1.4B qualitatively reproduces the 410M phase-diagram structure---detection boundary at $\\alpha_c{\\approx}0.50$, cross-$T$ improvement at 3/4 $\\alpha$ values ($\\alpha{=}0.45$: $0/6{\\to}5/6$ from $T{=}10$ to $T{=}15$)---though the 410M sync-dip at $\\alpha{=}0.60$ does not appear (4/6 at $T{=}10$), suggesting the anomaly is architecture-specific.\nThe four non-confirming conditions each isolate a distinct PermG assumption violation.\n\\emph{Precision sensitivity}: GPT-2 bf16 yields 0/8 vs.\\ fp32 6/8, traced to bf16 quantization noise masking the entropy signal.\n\\emph{Bidirectional confounding}: GPT-2 s43 detects 6/8 in both forward and reverse directions, preventing directional assignment.\n\\emph{Seed dependence}: Pythia-1.4B shows s42 2/8 vs.\\ s43 6/8; directional asymmetry is seed-specific (s42 reverse 0/6 across all conditions; s43 reverse 6/6 at $\\alpha{\\geq}0.60$).\n\\emph{Entropy decoupling}: SmolLM3-3B reaches only 3/8, with ECE-only detection (entropy 0/4), violating the entropy-leads assumption of Design Principle~\\ref{thm:entropy_leads}.\nStandalone PermG claims therefore require architecture-specific null calibration; GPT-2 null FPR reaches 50\\% (Appendix~\\ref{app:fpr_independence}), driven by ${\\sim}4{\\times}$ stronger null drift than Pythia.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/fig_cross_arch_heatmap.pdf}\n\\caption{Cross-architecture validation ($\\alpha{=}1.0$): PermG forward detection (${\\geq}6/8$) in 11/15 model-seed conditions spanning five families (345M--6.9B); faded bars: non-confirming conditions.}\n\\label{fig:cross_arch_heatmap}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\n\\subsection{Canary-Based Intervention (Proof of Concept)}\n\\label{sec:intervention}\n\nAs a proof-of-concept under favorable conditions ($\\alpha{=}1.0$), we implement predictive cessation: training switches to pure real data when token entropy drops beyond a threshold from generation~0.\nThe 15\\% threshold lies within a robust band $[10\\%, 22\\%]$ where all thresholds trigger at the same generation (Appendix~\\ref{app:intervention_sensitivity}); thresholds below 10\\% risk false triggering from normal fine-tuning drift, while those above 22\\% delay intervention past the diversity-loss onset.\n\nThe predictive trigger fires at generation~1, one full generation before a reactive baseline that monitors distinct-1 directly.\nThe reactive baseline requires observing an actual diversity drop, which manifests only at generation~2 due to the cascade lag (Design Principle~\\ref{prop:div_lag}); by then, perplexity has already risen to $157.09$.\nPredictive cessation caps peak perplexity at $56.99$, a 64\\% reduction (Figure~\\ref{fig:intervention}).\nA naive PPL threshold also triggers at generation~1 due to the large PPL jump ($+113\\%$), but requires a held-out evaluation set; entropy is a zero-cost byproduct of generation (Appendix~\\ref{app:ppl_threshold}).\nThis advantage replicates on Pythia-1.4B with comparable effect size, confirming that the entropy-leads-diversity ordering transfers across scales within the Pythia family.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/fig_intervention.pdf}\n\\caption{Canary-triggered intervention (Pythia-410M, $\\alpha{=}1.0$). Predictive cessation on entropy (generation~1) reduces peak perplexity by 64\\% versus reactive triggering on distinct-1 (generation~2).}\n\\label{fig:intervention}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\caption{Consensus threshold operating characteristics ($K \\in \\{2,3,4,5\\}$). FPR: null-control rate (10~seeds, 60~pairs; $K{\\geq}2$ uses 9-seed estimate). TPR: detection rate at selected $\\alpha$ levels (Pythia-410M, $T{=}10$, fp32). $K{=}3$ balances FPR control against detection power (75\\% at $\\alpha{=}1.0$); $K{=}4$ eliminates false positives but reduces TPR to ${\\leq}25\\%$.}\n\\label{tab:mmct_k_operating}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}lcccc@{}}\n\\toprule\n& $K{\\geq}2$ & $K{\\geq}3$ & $K{\\geq}4$ & $K{=}5$ \\\\\n\\midrule\nFPR ($\\alpha{=}0.0$) & 38.9\\% & 10.0\\% & 0\\% & 0\\% \\\\\nTPR ($\\alpha{=}0.50$) & 85.4\\% & 66.7\\% & 25.0\\% & 4.2\\% \\\\\nTPR ($\\alpha{=}0.75$) & 72.9\\% & 37.5\\% & 0\\% & 0\\% \\\\\nTPR ($\\alpha{=}1.0$) & 75.0\\% & 75.0\\% & 25.0\\% & 0\\% \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n",

The appendix has more flexibility for layout adjustments, so I should focus there. Page 25 actually looks fine now with the three figures distributed across columns. The remaining issues are mostly acceptable—text-only pages for methodology and reproducibility sections, one table on page 30, and the nearly empty final page which is typical for papers. The last page mainly contains the tail end of the AI assistants section, so there's limited room to improve without restructuring content.

Let me reconsider the user's requirement more carefully—every page except the first needs at least one figure or table. I should systematically check which pages in the main text are missing visuals, since pages 15, 30, and 35 are text-heavy and harder to fix. Page 36 being nearly empty is the most pressing issue to address.

For page 25, I can see the three figures are reasonably distributed across the layout. Let me focus on whether I can strategically move floats in the appendix to push content around and fill those text-only pages, or if I should just compile and deliver what I have since some of these constraints are difficult to work around.

828	\paragraph{Interpretation.}
829	The FPR-inverse weighting scheme (\S\ref{sec:mmct}) penalizes high-FPR methods by assigning weight $w_k = 1/\max(\widehat{\mathrm{FPR}}_k, \epsilon)$.
830	Under this scheme, NaiveXCorr (FPR${=}94.4\%$) receives weight $w{=}1.06$, while PermG (FPR${=}6.7\%$) receives $w{=}14.9$---a $14{\times}$ differential.
831	Equal weighting gives all methods identical influence, which reduces PermG's advantage but does not change its ranking among directional methods.
832	The invariance across weighting schemes (Table~\ref{tab:weight_robustness}) confirms that PermG's superiority reflects genuine detection properties rather than favorable weighting.
833	
834	\paragraph{Per-pair contribution variability.}
835	Marginal contributions vary substantially across canary$\to$downstream pairs (Table~\ref{tab:shapley_per_pair}).
836	Token entropy$\to$distinct-1 produces the most consistent positive contribution across coalitions (std${=}0.021$), while ECE$\to$perplexity shows the highest variance (std${=}0.089$), reflecting the contemporaneous coupling issue (\S\ref{app:channel_effect_size}).
837

839	\centering
840	\small
841	\caption{Shapley contribution by canary$\to$downstream pair (PermG, 6-pair basis). $\phi^{\text{FNR}}$ = FNR reduction (more negative = better). Std = standard deviation across all $2^4$ coalitions that include this pair.}

851	Entropy$\to$PPL & $-0.002$ & 0.067 \\

856	
857	The four diversity-channel pairs collectively account for 96.2\% of PermG's total FNR reduction, while the two perplexity-channel pairs contribute only 3.8\%---consistent with the channel effect size analysis (\S\ref{app:channel_effect_size}) showing that perplexity channels lack the temporal lead structure required for Granger detection.
858	This concentration motivates a practical simplification: for deployment scenarios where computational cost matters, testing only the four diversity-channel pairs sacrifices less than 4\% of detection sensitivity while halving the multiple testing burden (4~tests instead of 8, reducing the FDR correction penalty).
859	
860	\paragraph{Coalition stability.}
861	PermG's marginal contribution is stable across coalition sizes: when added to a 1-method coalition, its average combined Shapley is $-0.138$ (SE${=}0.015$); when added to a 4-method coalition, $-0.144$ (SE${=}0.008$).
862	The near-zero variation ($\Delta{=}0.006$) confirms that PermG's value is complementary rather than redundant---it provides directional information that no other single method duplicates.
863	DiffXCorr shows the opposite pattern: its marginal contribution \emph{decreases} from $-0.312$ (1-method coalition) to $-0.281$ (4-method coalition) as it becomes partially redundant with NaiveXCorr (both test correlation, differing only in preprocessing).

959	The concentration of false positives in token-entropy$\to$distinct pairs at seeds~45 and 50 suggests stochastic null-condition coupling in these specific initializations rather than a systematic calibration failure.

961	This decomposition clarifies why $K{\geq}3$ FPR exceeds standalone PermG FPR: the consensus mechanism aggregates false positives from multiple methods rather than filtering them.
962	
963	\FloatBarrier
964	\section{Sigmoid Transition Shape}
965	\label{app:sigmoid_shape}
966	
967	Sigmoid regression on the original 13-point $T{=}10$ grid yields $\alpha_c = 0.483 \pm 0.006$ but $k = 478 \pm 475$ ($\mathrm{SE}(k) \approx k$), indicating that the coarse grid cannot constrain the transition shape.
968	Dense $\alpha$ sampling (17~values at $T{=}10$) resolves this ambiguity: formal likelihood-ratio testing strongly favors a smooth sigmoid over a literal step function ($\Delta\mathrm{AIC}{=}-110$), with fitted steepness $k{=}15.7$ (95\% CI $[9.4, 28.2]$).
969	The transition is steep but finite-width, not a literal discontinuity.
970	This observed steepness exceeds what smooth signal models predict: VAR(1) simulations under H0 (linear, $k{=}6.1$) and H0$'$ (concave, $k{=}4.9$) produce onset sigmoids with $k{=}4.9$--$6.1$ (\S\ref{app:power_sim}), yielding steepness ratios $k_{\mathrm{obs}} / k_{\mathrm{H0}} \approx 2.6$--$3.2$ (CI lower bound ratio ${\approx}2$).
971	
972	\paragraph{Combined $T{=}10 + T{=}20$ fit.}
973	Fitting a single sigmoid to both $T$ values yields $k{=}279.3$ (SE${=}527.6$), $\alpha_c{=}0.451$, $y_{\max}{=}0.75$, AIC${=}79.86$.
974	The 90\% transition width narrows from $[0.478, 0.487]$ (width$=0.0092$) at $T{=}10$ alone to $[0.443, 0.459]$ (width$=0.0158$) in the combined fit---a leftward shift of $\alpha_c$ by $0.031$ reflecting the $T{=}20$ data's detection at $\alpha{=}0.45$.
975	The combined $k$ drops by 41.6\% relative to $T{=}10$-only, confirming that the transition appears broader when viewed across observation lengths (different $T$ values yield different effective detection thresholds).
976	We ultimately do not report the combined sigmoid in the main text because $\alpha{=}0.45$ yields contradictory counts (0/8 at $T{=}10$ vs.\ 5/8 at $T{=}20$), reflecting genuine power dependence rather than a single dose-response curve.
977	
978	\paragraph{Alternative functional forms.}
979	Probit and sigmoid fits produce identical AIC (32.99 at $T{=}10$, 79.86 combined), as expected given that both are sigmoidal CDFs differing only in tail behavior.
```

{
  "type": "queue-operation",
  "operation": "enqueue",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-25T07:52:14.153Z",
  "sessionId": "36cb22b8-86b0-4028-b0c2-9fe60f48bf51",
  "content": "[飞书用户 陈卓凡 (ou_eb2177d32728ad89a7f84ac418c35184)]: 22,23,30页有留白要及时往上提，figure 16的几个面板不用处于同一页，可以跨页布置\n\n---\n\n[审稿报告]\nOverall: 3.0 (Findings)\n\n# Round 15 审稿报告 — Distrib Canary Collapse\n\n## Summary\n\n本文研究 iterative model collapse 场景下多种统计检测方法的灵敏度差异，以合成数据替换比例 α 和训练代数 T 为二维参数，构建 \"detectability landscape\"。核心发现包括：(1) 不同检测方法的 FPR 跨越 14 倍（6.7%–94.4%），方法选择是检测结论变异的首要来源；(2) α≈0.45 附近存在 detection boundary 的 phase transition；(3) α=0.60 存在 non-monotonic detection anomaly。论文提出 MMCT（Multi-Method Consensus Testing）框架进行方法融合，并在 α=1.0 条件下进行了 5 个模型家族的跨架构验证。\n\n## Strengths\n\n**S1. 系统性的多方法比较框架。** 论文在同一数据集上对 5 种检测方法进行系统比较（Section 3.3, Figure 2），1,160 个 specification 的 robustness analysis 揭示了 κ=0.185 的低 inter-method agreement。这种\"同一数据、多种方法、量化一致性\"的实证设计在 model collapse 文献中尚属首次，为该领域的方法论规范化提供了参考。\n\n**S2. Non-monotonic detection anomaly 的识别与分析。** α=0.60 处的 detection dip（Figure 3, Table 6）超越了简单的 dose-response 单调关系，Section 4.2 和 Appendix K 对其成因（entropy-diversity temporal decoupling）的分析展示了超出参数扫描的结构性发现。\n\n**S3. 方法论警示的实用价值。** NaiveXCorr 的 FPR 高达 94.4%（检出 7-8/8 null pairs）源于 shared trend confounding（Section 3.3），这一 domain-specific 陷阱对从业者有直接的指导意义——大多数 practitioner 会首先尝试简单 cross-correlation，论文提供了明确的反面教材和替代方案（first-differencing, PermG）。\n\n**S4. 实验规模和透明度。** 涵盖 5 个模型家族（345M–6.9B），15 个 model-seed 组合，Appendix 提供了详尽的消融分析（Shapley contribution, weight robustness, power simulation, CUSUM comparison 等），AI 使用披露清晰（page 37，三种使用类别）。\n\n**S5. 写作质量和格式合规。** 论文格式符合 ACL 模板，booktabs 表格规范，LaTeX 无编译错误，Limitations section 覆盖 3 个要点（Scope, Statistical power, Causal interpretation），符合 EMNLP 2026 要求。\n\n## Weaknesses — Must-Fix\n\n**MF1. [共识] 跨架构泛化仅在 α=1.0，landscape 泛化性不足。**\nSection 4.5 (Table 1, page 6) 的跨架构验证仅在 α=1.0（全量合成替换）条件下进行，而论文最核心的发现——detection boundary 附近的 phase transition（α≈0.45）——没有任何跨架构验证。4/15 non-confirming 条件（Table 10, page 17）中 SmolLM3-3B 的 entropy decoupling 和 GPT-2 Medium 的 calibration dependency 暗示 landscape 是 architecture-dependent 的。论文标题 \"Characterizing the Detectability Landscape\" 暗示普适性，但实际上是 Pythia-410M 的 landscape。[R1-N2, R2-N2: 双方 AGREE]\n\n**MF2. [共识] Power-law boundary 拟合严重欠定。**\nSection 4.3 的 sigmoid regression 95% CI 为 [0.25, 0.82]，Limitations 自承 γ 的 CI 为 [0.01, 2.10]——跨越两个数量级。3 个自由参数拟合 3-4 个数据点是插值而非回归，对 power-law 的函数形式没有约束力。Appendix Y 的 power simulation 依赖同样的参数化形式，构成循环论证。需要至少 6-8 个 α 水平的数据点才有统计意义。[R1-N1, R2 AGREE]\n\n**MF3. [共识] PermG FPR 6.7% 置信区间过宽，削弱核心结论。**\n60 null pairs 下 4/60 = 6.7% 的 exact binomial 95% CI 约为 [1.8%, 16.2%]。CI 上界 16.2% 与 DiffXCorr 的 14.8% 实测 FPR 重叠，使 PermG 相对于 DiffXCorr 的 FPR 优势在统计上不显著。Conclusion 中 \"combining the lowest FPR 6.7%\" 给出了比数据实际支持的更精确的印象。应在正文中明确报告 CI。[R1-N3, R2 AGREE with nuance——R2 补充点估计 ranking 方向仍有信息价值，但不应作为精确数字使用]\n\n**MF4. [分歧] \"Detectability landscape\" 概念新颖性。**\nR2 认为 phase diagram 本质上是标准 dose-response 参数扫描加 sigmoid 拟合，\"landscape\" 是术语通胀。R1 部分反驳：multi-method comparison（1,160 specifications, κ=0.185）和 non-monotonic anomaly（α=0.60）超越了标准 dose-response，但同意 \"landscape\" 术语确实有 inflation，建议改为 \"multi-method detection sensitivity profile\"。\n→ 共识方向：landscape 的概念框架需要降低 claim 强度，但 multi-method comparison 和 non-monotonic finding 是有实质内容的。[R1 REBUT with concessions, R2-N1]\n\n**MF5. [分歧] Method sensitivity 作为 \"primary finding\" 的定位。**\nR2 认为\"不同统计检验具有不同 power 和 Type I error\"是统计学常识，不构成发现。R1 反驳：14 倍 FPR 差异（6.7%→94.4%）是 domain-specific 实证发现，且 NaiveXCorr 的 shared trend confounding 是 model collapse 时序数据的特有陷阱。但 R1 也同意将此定位为 \"primary finding\"（Contribution 1）确实偏高，建议降为 \"important methodological warning\"。\n→ 共识方向：FPR 差异幅度有信息价值，但 Contribution 列表应重新排序。[R1 REBUT with concessions, R2-N3]\n\n## Weaknesses — Should-Fix\n\n**SF1. [共识] 3 seeds 不足以估计 seed-level variance。**\nPhase diagram 用 3 seeds（Figure 3 caption），3 seeds 的标准差估计服从 2 自由度的 χ² 分布，CI 极宽。Appendix L 的跨 seed 分析也仅 3-4 seeds，无法从根本上改善估计精度。[R1-W1, R2 AGREE]\n\n**SF2. [共识] MMCT 方法论贡献薄弱，增量价值有限。**\nAlgorithm 1 (page 4) 的 FPR-inverse weighted majority voting 在 ensemble learning 文献中是基本技术。Appendix P Shapley 分析显示 PermG 的 combined Shapley 最接近零（FPR-FNR 最平衡），MMCT 的主要价值是排除高 FPR 方法而非组合互补方法。Table 2 已显示 PermG 单独是最优选择时，MMCT 增量价值有限。w_k = 1/max(FPR_k, ε) 是直觉合理但缺乏理论最优性保证的 ad-hoc 设计。[R1-W2, R2-W1: 双方 AGREE]\n\n**SF3. [共识] Intervention 仅在 α=1.0 验证但占据过多正文篇幅。**\nSection 4.6 (page 7) 的 intervention 仅在 α=1.0 验证，但占正文约 0.5 页。Appendix F (CUSUM comparison) 显示在高 α 下 CUSUM 与 canary 方法差异不大，削弱了 canary-based intervention 的独特价值主张。Discussion 的 \"deployment recommendations\" 超出了单一极端条件的证据支撑。建议正文压缩到 1-2 段，细节移入附录。[R1-W3, R2-W3: 双方 AGREE]\n\n**SF4. [共识] Entropy \"leading\" 可能是测量分辨率差异的 artifact。**\nAppendix J (page 17) 自己承认 entropy 是连续量而 distinct-n 是离散计数，entropy 可以响应任意小的分布变化。这是对 Design Principle 1（entropy precedes diversity）的根本性替代解释，但被放在 appendix 而非 Section 5 Discussion 中。需要：(a) 在 Discussion 中明确讨论此 concern，(b) 对 entropy 进行 discretization 后重新测试 Granger precedence 是否依然成立（论文未做此验证）。[R1-W4, R2-W4: 双方 AGREE]\n\n**SF5. [共识] 缺少 Silberzahn et al. (2018) \"Many Analysts, One Data Set\"。**\n论文的 core finding（method choice as primary variability source）直接回应了 Silberzahn 框架。Section 2 或 Section 3.3 应引用并讨论。[R2-W2, R1 AGREE]\n\n**SF6. [部分共识] 核心内容过度依赖附录。**\nR2 认为 Design Principle 推导、方法论比较表等放入附录导致正文不 self-contained。R1 反驳：8 页限制下这是合理选择，但同意正文应增加 1-2 句 intuitive justification（如 Section 3.1 的 Design Principle 缺少直觉解释）。[R1 partial REBUT, R2-W5]\n\n## Reviewer Consensus\n\n| 问题 | R1 | R2 | 共识 |\n|------|----|----|------|\n| 跨架构仅 α=1.0 | N2 | N2 | **AGREE** — 最高共识 Must-Fix |\n| Boundary fitting 欠定 | N1 | AGREE | **AGREE** — 3 参数拟合 3-4 点 = 插值 |\n| PermG FPR CI 过宽 | N3 | AGREE | **AGREE** — CI 上界与 DiffXCorr 重叠 |\n| 3 seeds 不足 | W1 | AGREE | **AGREE** |\n| MMCT 增量价值有限 | W2 | W1 | **AGREE** |\n| Intervention 过于初步 | W3 | W3 | **AGREE** |\n| Entropy artifact 应在主文讨论 | W4 | W4 | **AGREE** |\n| 缺失 Silberzahn (2018) | — | W2 | **AGREE** |\n| Table 2 K≥4/K≥5 列冗余 | P1 | AGREE | **AGREE** |\n| Figure 1 低值区颜色区分度差 | P2 | AGREE | **AGREE** |\n| Design Principle 正文缺中间推导 | P3 | AGREE | **AGREE** |\n| PermG direction checking 正文不足 | P4 | AGREE | **AGREE** |\n\n## Reviewer Divergence\n\n| 问题 | R1 立场 | R2 立场 | 分歧性质 |\n|------|---------|---------|----------|\n| Landscape = dose-response? | REBUT — multi-method + non-monotonic 超越 dose-response，但同意术语 inflation | N1 — 本质是参数扫描 rebranding | 程度分歧：方向一致（需降低 claim），程度不同 |\n| Method sensitivity 新颖性 | REBUT — 14x FPR 差异是 domain-specific 发现 | N3 — 统计学常识 | 程度分歧：R1 认为有实证价值但 framing 偏高 |\n| 附录依赖 | partial REBUT — 页面约束下合理 | W5 — 正文不 self-contained | 程度分歧：R1 主张结构合理但需增加直觉解释 |\n| AI disclosure 详细度 | P5 — 不够详细 | REBUT — page 37 已充分 | 事实分歧：R2 指出 page 37 已列出三种使用类别 |\n\n## Questions\n\n**Q1.** 如果在 GPT-2 Medium 或 SmolLM3-3B 上做 α∈{0.25, 0.50, 0.75} 的完整扫描，phase boundary 位置是否与 Pythia-410M 的 α≈0.45 一致？如果不一致，论文的定量 claim 需要如何重写？\n\n**Q2.** 排除 NaiveXCorr 后，specification curve 的 κ 值是多少？如果显著提高，method sensitivity 的 claim 是否需要重新校准？\n\n**Q3.** 对 entropy 进行 discretization（如 binning 到与 distinct-n 相同的分辨率）后重新运行 Granger precedence test，Design Principle 1 是否仍然成立？\n\n**Q4.** 能否提供 MMCT 确实优于单独使用 PermG 的具体场景？如果没有这样的场景，MMCT 的方法论 claim 是否应该降级？\n\n## Missing References\n\n1. **Silberzahn et al. (2018)** \"Many Analysts, One Data Set: Making Transparent How Variations in Analytic Choices Affect Results\" — 多方法/多分析者范式的奠基性工作，论文的 method sensitivity finding 直接回应此框架。[R1 AGREE, R2 AGREE] **应引用。**\n\n2. **Kirchenbauer et al. (2023)** \"A Watermark for Large Language Models\" — watermarking 作为 proactive detection 的替代范式，与本文的 reactive distributional detection 形成方法论区分。Related Work 应简要讨论两者的差异。[R2 AGREE] **应引用。**\n\n3. DetectGPT (Mitchell et al., 2023) — R1 指出问题设定完全不同（per-text classification vs. distributional detection on time series），论文已在 Section 2.2 划清边界。**非必须。**\n\n4. MMD / Gretton et al. (2012) — R1 指出 MMD 在本文 time-series lead-lag 设定中的适用性不明确。**非必须。**\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n未请求外部审稿。\n\n## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）\n\n视觉审查已完成。0 critical / 7 warnings / 9 info。\n\n**Warnings:**\n1. **Page 2**: Figure 1 phase diagram 单元格数字标注字号偏小；红-橙-黄-绿渐变对红绿色盲不友好，建议 viridis/Okabe-Ito 色板\n2. **Page 5**: 单页 3 个 display items（Figure 3 + Figure 4 + Table 1），Figure 4 子面板轴标签约正文字号 50-60%，低于 70% 可读性阈值\n3. **Page 6**: 单页 3 个 figures（Figure 5-7），视觉压缩严重\n4. **Page 7**: Figure 8 条形图 y 轴模型名称字号偏小\n5. **Page 20**: Figure 14 占 75% 页面，底部 25% 空白\n6. **Page 24**: Figures 16-17 后底部 30% 空白\n7. **Page 37**: 最后一页仅 ~6 行文字，底部 85% 空白\n\n**Info highlights:**\n- 首页无视觉锚点（建议非必须）\n- 正文 12 个 display items 略高于 8 页典型 5-8 个；Figure 3 与 Figure 7 信息重叠可考虑合并\n- Limitations 覆盖 3 点，篇幅适中，符合 EMNLP 2026\n- 表格 booktabs 规范，公式正确，无 margin 溢出，References 统一\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**评分：3.0 (Findings)**\n\n**置信度：High**\n\nR1 给出 3.5（Borderline Conference），R2 给出 3.0（Findings）。差异来自对 \"detectability landscape\" 概念新颖性的评估——R2 认为即使修复拟合和泛化问题，landscape 的概念新颖性仍有限（本质是 dose-response rebranding），R1 认为 multi-method comparison 增加了超越 dose-response 的价值。\n\n综合评估采用 3.0，理由：\n\n1. **泛化性缺陷是根本性的。** 跨架构验证仅在 α=1.0（双方最高共识 Must-Fix），标题承诺的 \"landscape characterization\" 实际仅基于 Pythia-410M。需要额外实验而非仅修改写作。\n\n2. **统计精度不足以支撑定量 claim。** Boundary fitting 欠定（3 参数 / 3-4 点）、PermG FPR CI 过宽（[1.8%, 16.2%]）、3 seeds 的 variance 估计——共同使得定量结论（\"α_c ≈ 0.45\"、\"FPR = 6.7%\"）的 precision 远低于呈现方式所暗示的。\n\n3. **MMCT 方法论贡献薄弱。** 在 PermG 已是最优单方法的情况下，MMCT 的增量价值缺乏证据支持，且缺乏理论最优性保证。\n\n4. **实证发现有真正价值。** 14 倍 FPR 差异的定量化、NaiveXCorr 的 shared trend confounding 警示、α=0.60 non-monotonic anomaly——这些是 genuine contributions，适合 Findings 收录。\n\n**ARR 评分对照：Overall Assessment = 3 (Findings)** — 可被 Findings 接收，但需显著修改 framing 和补充跨架构实验后才能考虑主会议。\n\n## Detailed Comments\n\n### Section 1 — Introduction\n- \"Characterizing the Detectability Landscape\" 标题暗示普适性，与实际仅基于 Pythia-410M 的证据不匹配\n- Contribution 列表应重新排序：method sensitivity 从 Contribution 1 降为 methodological warning，landscape characterization 需加 scope qualifier\n\n### Section 2 — Related Work\n- 缺少 Silberzahn et al. (2018) 和 Kirchenbauer et al. (2023) 的讨论\n- 与 Schaeffer et al. (2025) \"8 种 collapse 定义\" 的映射应更明确\n\n### Section 3 — Method\n- Design Principle 1 和 2 的 formal statement 使用信息论语言但 operationalization 跳转到具体 metrics 时缺少中间推导（仅引用 Appendix，主文无直觉解释）\n- MMCT Algorithm 1 的 FPR-inverse weighting 缺乏理论依据，Appendix Q \"对 ad-hoc 选择不敏感\" ≠ \"有理论依据\"\n- PermG direction checking 描述不足（Appendix G 有详细描述，但 Section 3.3 缺关键细节）\n\n### Section 4 — Experiments\n- Phase diagram boundary fitting：需要更多数据点（建议 ≥6 个 α 水平），当前 CI 过宽\n- Cross-architecture：Table 1 仅 α=1.0，需要至少 α∈{0.50, 0.75} 的验证\n- Intervention：正文篇幅应压缩，证据仅支持 proof-of-concept 定位\n- Table 2 的 K≥4 和 K≥5 列全为 0%，可删除或合并为脚注\n- α=0.60 non-monotonic anomaly 是有价值的发现，建议给予更多正文空间\n\n### Section 5 — Discussion\n- Entropy artifact（测量分辨率 vs. 因果 precedence）应从 Appendix J 提升到 Discussion\n- Design Principle 1 的因果叙事需要弱化，或提供 discretized entropy 的验证\n\n### Section 6 — Conclusion\n- \"combining the lowest FPR 6.7%\" 应改为包含 CI 的表述\n- Cross-architecture generalization claim 需加 \"at α=1.0\" qualifier\n\n### Limitations\n- 当前 3 个点的覆盖适当，建议增加对 MMCT 增量价值有限的 honest assessment\n\n### Appendix\n- 内容全面，但部分关键验证（entropy artifact, PermG FPR CI）应在正文中有显式引用和简要讨论\n- Figure 子图内文字偏小问题（Page 5 Figure 4, Page 7 Figure 8）仍存在\n---\n\n## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)\n\n**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:\n\n1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),\n   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。\n\n2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:\n   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长\n   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补\n   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本\n   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据\n   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)\n\n3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:\n   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有\n   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的\n   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。\n\n4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,\n   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。\n   **对 CRITICAL/WARNING 级的修复指令, 必须要求 Agent 修改完成后把修后段落 +\n   原始审稿意见发给 Reviewer 做验证 — Reviewer 以审稿人视角判断修复是否充分,\n   返回 PASS 才算完成。** 不验证就标 resolved 是\"修了但没修对\"的根源。\n\n5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读\n   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标\n   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`\n   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。\n\n6. **区分写作问题和 evidence 问题**: 对每条 CRITICAL/WARNING 级审稿意见,\n   先判断: \"如果论文写得完美无缺(措辞零瑕疵),这个问题还存在吗?\"\n   - 是 → 这是 evidence 问题, 不能只改论文。必须补实验/补分析/降级 claim\n   - 否 → 这是写作问题, 可以通过改论文修复\n   审稿人说\"not convincing / insufficient evidence / lacks support / confound /\n   reliability / sample size\"时, 基本都是 evidence 问题 — 不要用加 hedging 应付。\n\n7. **检查复发问题**: 在制定修复方案之前, 先 `read_local_file` 读\n   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 找出本轮审稿意见中\n   哪些是之前已经出现过的老问题。对复发问题重点关注:\n   - 查看上轮用了什么方法修复(action_items 中记录了)\n   - 如果上轮已经用写作手段修复过但审稿人仍然 flag → 写作手段对这个问题无效,\n     必须换路径(补实验 / 补分析 / 降级 claim 位置)\n   - 在 Action Matrix 中标注 \"[复发 × N 轮]\", 并说明为什么这次的修复方案\n     和之前不同\n\n8. **claim 校准是双向的**: 实验结果超预期时 claim 可以升级(扩大 scope / 去掉\n   hedging / 提升到 headline), 实验结果不及预期时 claim 应该降级。降级 claim\n   不是降级论文 — 把一个 evidence 不足的 finding 从 headline 移到 discussion,\n   反而让论文整体更可信。\n\n9. **向 A 群汇报**: Action Matrix 定稿后,用 `director_reply_to_user` 向用户汇报:\n   - 审稿整体评估(系统性问题有哪些、严重程度)\n   - Action Matrix 摘要(补哪些实验、改哪些章节、写哪些 rebuttal)\n   - 预估时间线\n   - 需要用户决策的事项(如有)\n\n**禁止**:\n- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent\n- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action\n- 对低 priority 意见用\"暂不处理\"一笔带过而不给出明确理由\n- 整体性\"已知悉\"回复\n- 对 evidence 问题只用写作手段修复(第 6 条)\n- 对复发问题重复使用已失败的修复方法(第 7 条)\n"
}

[审稿报告]
Overall: 3.0 (Findings)

# Round 15 审稿报告 — Distrib Canary Collapse

## Summary

本文研究 iterative model collapse 场景下多种统计检测方法的灵敏度差异，以合成数据替换比例 α 和训练代数 T 为二维参数，构建 "detectability landscape"。核心发现包括：(1) 不同检测方法的 FPR 跨越 14 倍（6.7%–94.4%），方法选择是检测结论变异的首要来源；(2) α≈0.45 附近存在 detection boundary 的 phase transition；(3) α=0.60 存在 non-monotonic detection anomaly。论文提出 MMCT（Multi-Method Consensus Testing）框架进行方法融合，并在 α=1.0 条件下进行了 5 个模型家族的跨架构验证。

## Strengths

**S1. 系统性的多方法比较框架。** 论文在同一数据集上对 5 种检测方法进行系统比较（Section 3.3, Figure 2），1,160 个 specification 的 robustness analysis 揭示了 κ=0.185 的低 inter-method agreement。这种"同一数据、多种方法、量化一致性"的实证设计在 model collapse 文献中尚属首次，为该领域的方法论规范化提供了参考。

**S2. Non-monotonic detection anomaly 的识别与分析。** α=0.60 处的 detection dip（Figure 3, Table 6）超越了简单的 dose-response 单调关系，Section 4.2 和 Appendix K 对其成因（entropy-diversity temporal decoupling）的分析展示了超出参数扫描的结构性发现。

**S3. 方法论警示的实用价值。** NaiveXCorr 的 FPR 高达 94.4%（检出 7-8/8 null pairs）源于 shared trend confounding（Section 3.3），这一 domain-specific 陷阱对从业者有直接的指导意义——大多数 practitioner 会首先尝试简单 cross-correlation，论文提供了明确的反面教材和替代方案（first-differencing, PermG）。

**S4. 实验规模和透明度。** 涵盖 5 个模型家族（345M–6.9B），15 个 model-seed 组合，Appendix 提供了详尽的消融分析（Shapley contribution, weight robustness, power simulation, CUSUM comparison 等），AI 使用披露清晰（page 37，三种使用类别）。

**S5. 写作质量和格式合规。** 论文格式符合 ACL 模板，booktabs 表格规范，LaTeX 无编译错误，Limitations section 覆盖 3 个要点（Scope, Statistical power, Causal interpretation），符合 EMNLP 2026 要求。

## Weaknesses — Must-Fix

**MF1. [共识] 跨架构泛化仅在 α=1.0，landscape 泛化性不足。**
Section 4.5 (Table 1, page 6) 的跨架构验证仅在 α=1.0（全量合成替换）条件下进行，而论文最核心的发现——detection boundary 附近的 phase transition（α≈0.45）——没有任何跨架构验证。4/15 non-confirming 条件（Table 10, page 17）中 SmolLM3-3B 的 entropy decoupling 和 GPT-2 Medium 的 calibration dependency 暗示 landscape 是 architecture-dependent 的。论文标题 "Characterizing the Detectability Landscape" 暗示普适性，但实际上是 Pythia-410M 的 landscape。[R1-N2, R2-N2: 双方 AGREE]

**MF2. [共识] Power-law boundary 拟合严重欠定。**
Section 4.3 的 sigmoid regression 95% CI 为 [0.25, 0.82]，Limitations 自承 γ 的 CI 为 [0.01, 2.10]——跨越两个数量级。3 个自由参数拟合 3-4 个数据点是插值而非回归，对 power-law 的函数形式没有约束力。Appendix Y 的 power simulation 依赖同样的参数化形式，构成循环论证。需要至少 6-8 个 α 水平的数据点才有统计意义。[R1-N1, R2 AGREE]

**MF3. [共识] PermG FPR 6.7% 置信区间过宽，削弱核心结论。**
60 null pairs 下 4/60 = 6.7% 的 exact binomial 95% CI 约为 [1.8%, 16.2%]。CI 上界 16.2% 与 DiffXCorr 的 14.8% 实测 FPR 重叠，使 PermG 相对于 DiffXCorr 的 FPR 优势在统计上不显著。Conclusion 中 "combining the lowest FPR 6.7%" 给出了比数据实际支持的更精确的印象。应在正文中明确报告 CI。[R1-N3, R2 AGREE with nuance——R2 补充点估计 ranking 方向仍有信息价值，但不应作为精确数字使用]

**MF4. [分歧] "Detectability landscape" 概念新颖性。**
R2 认为 phase diagram 本质上是标准 dose-response 参数扫描加 sigmoid 拟合，"landscape" 是术语通胀。R1 部分反驳：multi-method comparison（1,160 specifications, κ=0.185）和 non-monotonic anomaly（α=0.60）超越了标准 dose-response，但同意 "landscape" 术语确实有 inflation，建议改为 "multi-method detection sensitivity profile"。
→ 共识方向：landscape 的概念框架需要降低 claim 强度，但 multi-method comparison 和 non-monotonic finding 是有实质内容的。[R1 REBUT with concessions, R2-N1]

**MF5. [分歧] Method sensitivity 作为 "primary finding" 的定位。**
R2 认为"不同统计检验具有不同 power 和 Type I error"是统计学常识，不构成发现。R1 反驳：14 倍 FPR 差异（6.7%→94.4%）是 domain-specific 实证发现，且 NaiveXCorr 的 shared trend confounding 是 model collapse 时序数据的特有陷阱。但 R1 也同意将此定位为 "primary finding"（Contribution 1）确实偏高，建议降为 "important methodological warning"。
→ 共识方向：FPR 差异幅度有信息价值，但 Contribution 列表应重新排序。[R1 REBUT with concessions, R2-N3]

## Weaknesses — Should-Fix

**SF1. [共识] 3 seeds 不足以估计 seed-level variance。**
Phase diagram 用 3 seeds（Figure 3 caption），3 seeds 的标准差估计服从 2 自由度的 χ² 分布，CI 极宽。Appendix L 的跨 seed 分析也仅 3-4 seeds，无法从根本上改善估计精度。[R1-W1, R2 AGREE]

**SF2. [共识] MMCT 方法论贡献薄弱，增量价值有限。**
Algorithm 1 (page 4) 的 FPR-inverse weighted majority voting 在 ensemble learning 文献中是基本技术。Appendix P Shapley 分析显示 PermG 的 combined Shapley 最接近零（FPR-FNR 最平衡），MMCT 的主要价值是排除高 FPR 方法而非组合互补方法。Table 2 已显示 PermG 单独是最优选择时，MMCT 增量价值有限。w_k = 1/max(FPR_k, ε) 是直觉合理但缺乏理论最优性保证的 ad-hoc 设计。[R1-W2, R2-W1: 双方 AGREE]

**SF3. [共识] Intervention 仅在 α=1.0 验证但占据过多正文篇幅。**
Section 4.6 (page 7) 的 intervention 仅在 α=1.0 验证，但占正文约 0.5 页。Appendix F (CUSUM comparison) 显示在高 α 下 CUSUM 与 canary 方法差异不大，削弱了 canary-based intervention 的独特价值主张。Discussion 的 "deployment recommendations" 超出了单一极端条件的证据支撑。建议正文压缩到 1-2 段，细节移入附录。[R1-W3, R2-W3: 双方 AGREE]

**SF4. [共识] Entropy "leading" 可能是测量分辨率差异的 artifact。**
Appendix J (page 17) 自己承认 entropy 是连续量而 distinct-n 是离散计数，entropy 可以响应任意小的分布变化。这是对 Design Principle 1（entropy precedes diversity）的根本性替代解释，但被放在 appendix 而非 Section 5 Discussion 中。需要：(a) 在 Discussion 中明确讨论此 concern，(b) 对 entropy 进行 discretization 后重新测试 Granger precedence 是否依然成立（论文未做此验证）。[R1-W4, R2-W4: 双方 AGREE]

**SF5. [共识] 缺少 Silberzahn et al. (2018) "Many Analysts, One Data Set"。**
论文的 core finding（method choice as primary variability source）直接回应了 Silberzahn 框架。Section 2 或 Section 3.3 应引用并讨论。[R2-W2, R1 AGREE]

**SF6. [部分共识] 核心内容过度依赖附录。**
R2 认为 Design Principle 推导、方法论比较表等放入附录导致正文不 self-contained。R1 反驳：8 页限制下这是合理选择，但同意正文应增加 1-2 句 intuitive justification（如 Section 3.1 的 Design Principle 缺少直觉解释）。[R1 partial REBUT, R2-W5]

## Questions

**Q1.** 如果在 GPT-2 Medium 或 SmolLM3-3B 上做 α∈{0.25, 0.50, 0.75} 的完整扫描，phase boundary 位置是否与 Pythia-410M 的 α≈0.45 一致？如果不一致，论文的定量 claim 需要如何重写？

**Q2.** 排除 NaiveXCorr 后，specification curve 的 κ 值是多少？如果显著提高，method sensitivity 的 claim 是否需要重新校准？

**Q3.** 对 entropy 进行 discretization（如 binning 到与 distinct-n 相同的分辨率）后重新运行 Granger precedence test，Design Principle 1 是否仍然成立？

**Q4.** 能否提供 MMCT 确实优于单独使用 PermG 的具体场景？如果没有这样的场景，MMCT 的方法论 claim 是否应该降级？

## Missing References

1. **Silberzahn et al. (2018)** "Many Analysts, One Data Set: Making Transparent How Variations in Analytic Choices Affect Results" — 多方法/多分析者范式的奠基性工作，论文的 method sensitivity finding 直接回应此框架。[R1 AGREE, R2 AGREE] **应引用。**

2. **Kirchenbauer et al. (2023)** "A Watermark for Large Language Models" — watermarking 作为 proactive detection 的替代范式，与本文的 reactive distributional detection 形成方法论区分。Related Work 应简要讨论两者的差异。[R2 AGREE] **应引用。**

3. DetectGPT (Mitchell et al., 2023) — R1 指出问题设定完全不同（per-text classification vs. distributional detection on time series），论文已在 Section 2.2 划清边界。**非必须。**

4. MMD / Gretton et al. (2012) — R1 指出 MMD 在本文 time-series lead-lag 设定中的适用性不明确。**非必须。**

## Overall Assessment

**评分：3.0 (Findings)**

**置信度：High**

R1 给出 3.5（Borderline Conference），R2 给出 3.0（Findings）。差异来自对 "detectability landscape" 概念新颖性的评估——R2 认为即使修复拟合和泛化问题，landscape 的概念新颖性仍有限（本质是 dose-response rebranding），R1 认为 multi-method comparison 增加了超越 dose-response 的价值。

综合评估采用 3.0，理由：

1. **泛化性缺陷是根本性的。** 跨架构验证仅在 α=1.0（双方最高共识 Must-Fix），标题承诺的 "landscape characterization" 实际仅基于 Pythia-410M。需要额外实验而非仅修改写作。

2. **统计精度不足以支撑定量 claim。** Boundary fitting 欠定（3 参数 / 3-4 点）、PermG FPR CI 过宽（[1.8%, 16.2%]）、3 seeds 的 variance 估计——共同使得定量结论（"α_c ≈ 0.45"、"FPR = 6.7%"）的 precision 远低于呈现方式所暗示的。

3. **MMCT 方法论贡献薄弱。** 在 PermG 已是最优单方法的情况下，MMCT 的增量价值缺乏证据支持，且缺乏理论最优性保证。

4. **实证发现有真正价值。** 14 倍 FPR 差异的定量化、NaiveXCorr 的 shared trend confounding 警示、α=0.60 non-monotonic anomaly——这些是 genuine contributions，适合 Findings 收录。

**ARR 评分对照：Overall Assessment = 3 (Findings)** — 可被 Findings 接收，但需显著修改 framing 和补充跨架构实验后才能考虑主会议。

## Detailed Comments

### Section 1 — Introduction
- "Characterizing the Detectability Landscape" 标题暗示普适性，与实际仅基于 Pythia-410M 的证据不匹配
- Contribution 列表应重新排序：method sensitivity 从 Contribution 1 降为 methodological warning，landscape characterization 需加 scope qualifier

### Section 2 — Related Work
- 缺少 Silberzahn et al. (2018) 和 Kirchenbauer et al. (2023) 的讨论
- 与 Schaeffer et al. (2025) "8 种 collapse 定义" 的映射应更明确

### Section 3 — Method
- Design Principle 1 和 2 的 formal statement 使用信息论语言但 operationalization 跳转到具体 metrics 时缺少中间推导（仅引用 Appendix，主文无直觉解释）
- MMCT Algorithm 1 的 FPR-inverse weighting 缺乏理论依据，Appendix Q "对 ad-hoc 选择不敏感" ≠ "有理论依据"
- PermG direction checking 描述不足（Appendix G 有详细描述，但 Section 3.3 缺关键细节）

### Section 4 — Experiments
- Phase diagram boundary fitting：需要更多数据点（建议 ≥6 个 α 水平），当前 CI 过宽
- Cross-architecture：Table 1 仅 α=1.0，需要至少 α∈{0.50, 0.75} 的验证
- Intervention：正文篇幅应压缩，证据仅支持 proof-of-concept 定位
- Table 2 的 K≥4 和 K≥5 列全为 0%，可删除或合并为脚注
- α=0.60 non-monotonic anomaly 是有价值的发现，建议给予更多正文空间

### Section 5 — Discussion
- Entropy artifact（测量分辨率 vs. 因果 precedence）应从 Appendix J 提升到 Discussion
- Design Principle 1 的因果叙事需要弱化，或提供 discretized entropy 的验证

### Section 6 — Conclusion
- "combining the lowest FPR 6.7%" 应改为包含 CI 的表述
- Cross-architecture generalization claim 需加 "at α=1.0" qualifier

### Limitations
- 当前 3 个点的覆盖适当，建议增加对 MMCT 增量价值有限的 honest assessment

### Appendix
- 内容全面，但部分关键验证（entropy artifact, PermG FPR CI）应在正文中有显式引用和简要讨论
- Figure 子图内文字偏小问题（Page 5 Figure 4, Page 7 Figure 8）仍存在

## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)

**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:

1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),
   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。

2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:
   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长
   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补
   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本
   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据
   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)

3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:
   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有
   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的
   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。

4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,
   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。
   **对 CRITICAL/WARNING 级的修复指令, 必须要求 Agent 修改完成后把修后段落 +
   原始审稿意见发给 Reviewer 做验证 — Reviewer 以审稿人视角判断修复是否充分,
   返回 PASS 才算完成。** 不验证就标 resolved 是"修了但没修对"的根源。

5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标
   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`
   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。

6. **区分写作问题和 evidence 问题**: 对每条 CRITICAL/WARNING 级审稿意见,
   先判断: "如果论文写得完美无缺(措辞零瑕疵),这个问题还存在吗?"
   - 是 → 这是 evidence 问题, 不能只改论文。必须补实验/补分析/降级 claim
   - 否 → 这是写作问题, 可以通过改论文修复
   审稿人说"not convincing / insufficient evidence / lacks support / confound /
   reliability / sample size"时, 基本都是 evidence 问题 — 不要用加 hedging 应付。

7. **检查复发问题**: 在制定修复方案之前, 先 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 找出本轮审稿意见中
   哪些是之前已经出现过的老问题。对复发问题重点关注:
   - 查看上轮用了什么方法修复(action_items 中记录了)
   - 如果上轮已经用写作手段修复过但审稿人仍然 flag → 写作手段对这个问题无效,
     必须换路径(补实验 / 补分析 / 降级 claim 位置)
   - 在 Action Matrix 中标注 "[复发 × N 轮]", 并说明为什么这次的修复方案
     和之前不同

8. **claim 校准是双向的**: 实验结果超预期时 claim 可以升级(扩大 scope / 去掉
   hedging / 提升到 headline), 实验结果不及预期时 claim 应该降级。降级 claim
   不是降级论文 — 把一个 evidence 不足的 finding 从 headline 移到 discussion,
   反而让论文整体更可信。

9. **向 A 群汇报**: Action Matrix 定稿后,用 `director_reply_to_user` 向用户汇报:
   - 审稿整体评估(系统性问题有哪些、严重程度)
   - Action Matrix 摘要(补哪些实验、改哪些章节、写哪些 rebuttal)
   - 预估时间线
   - 需要用户决策的事项(如有)

**禁止**:
- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent
- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action
- 对低 priority 意见用"暂不处理"一笔带过而不给出明确理由
- 整体性"已知悉"回复
- 对 evidence 问题只用写作手段修复(第 6 条)
- 对复发问题重复使用已失败的修复方法(第 7 条)

771	\section{Shapley Value Analysis}
772	\label{app:shapley}
773	
774	Shapley values~\citep{shapley1953value} computed over all $2^5{=}32$ coalitions quantify each method's marginal contribution to FPR and FNR (Table~\ref{tab:shapley_6pair}).
775	DiffXCorr achieves the best combined Shapley ($-0.299$) but cannot separate forward from reverse; among directional methods, PermG ranks best ($-0.141$).

781	\caption{Shapley value decomposition by method. FPR contribution (positive = worse) and FNR contribution (negative = better) are shown for each of the five detection methods. DiffXCorr achieves the best combined Shapley ($-0.299$) but lacks directional separation; among directional methods, PermG ranks best ($-0.141$). NaiveXCorr contributes the most FPR ($+0.609$) due to its inability to distinguish signal from null drift.}
782	\label{fig:shapley_contribution}
783	\end{figure}

796	Threshold ($3\sigma$) & $+0.111$ & $-0.027$ & $+0.084$ \\

804	\section{MMCT Weight Robustness Ablation}
805	\label{app:weight_robustness}
806

808	Cap-10 and cap-100 yield identical values (consensus threshold saturates), and equal weighting still gives PermG the best combined Shapley ($-0.152$) among directional methods.

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01WCcaZ4YzVQ3UVzmm1fXNyF`</summary>

```text
1	"""
2	Shapley value decomposition: method contributions to FPR and FNR.
3	narrative_intent: "PermG provides the best FPR-FNR tradeoff among
4	directional methods; NaiveXCorr dominates FPR cost."
5	Uses v3 data (6 null seeds, 36 null observations).
6	"""
7	import matplotlib
8	matplotlib.use('Agg')
9	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
10	import numpy as np
11	import json
12	
13	plt.rcParams.update({
14	    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',
15	    'font.size': 9,
16	    'axes.titlesize': 10,
17	    'axes.labelsize': 9,
18	    'xtick.labelsize': 7.5,
19	    'ytick.labelsize': 8,
20	    'legend.fontsize': 7,
21	    'figure.dpi': 300,
22	    'savefig.dpi': 300,
23	    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',
24	    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.03,
25	    'axes.spines.top': False,
26	    'axes.spines.right': False,
27	    'pdf.fonttype': 42,
28	    'ps.fonttype': 42,
29	})
30	
31	with open('artifacts/shapley_v3/shapley_values_v3.json') as f:
32	    d = json.load(f)
33	
34	fpr = d['main_result_6pair']['fpr_shapley']
35	fnr = d['main_result_6pair']['fnr_shapley']
36	combined = d['main_result_6pair']['combined_shapley']
37	
38	method_order = ['naive_xcorr', 'diff_xcorr', 'threshold_onset', 'perm_granger', 'ty_granger']
39	method_labels = ['Naive\nXCorr', 'Diff.\nXCorr', 'Threshold\nOnset', 'Perm.\nGranger', 'Toda-\nYamamoto']
40	
41	colors_pos = '#B2182B'
42	colors_neg = '#1B7837'
43	hero_color = '#2166AC'
44	
45	fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(5.5, 2.5), sharey=False)
46	
47	datasets = [
48	    ('FPR contribution', fpr, 'increases\nFPR', 'reduces\nFPR'),
49	    ('FNR contribution', fnr, 'increases\nFNR', 'reduces\nFNR'),
50	    ('Combined (FPR+FNR)', combined, 'net\nharm', 'net\nbenefit'),
51	]
52	
53	x = np.arange(len(method_order))
54	
55	for ax, (title, vals_dict, pos_label, neg_label) in zip(axes, datasets):
56	    vals = [vals_dict[m] for m in method_order]
57	    bar_colors = []
58	    for i, (v, m) in enumerate(zip(vals, method_order)):
59	        if m == 'perm_granger':
60	            bar_colors.append(hero_color)
61	        elif v > 0:
62	            bar_colors.append(colors_pos)
63	        else:
64	            bar_colors.append(colors_neg)
65	
66	    bars = ax.bar(x, vals, 0.65, color=bar_colors, alpha=0.85,
67	                  edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.3)
68	
69	    # Value labels
70	    for i, v in enumerate(vals):
71	        offset = 0.01 if v >= 0 else -0.01
72	        va = 'bottom' if v >= 0 else 'top'
73	        ax.text(i, v + offset, f'{v:+.3f}', ha='center', va=va,
74	                fontsize=5.5, fontweight='bold' if method_order[i] == 'perm_granger' else 'normal')
75	
76	    ax.axhline(0, color='black', linewidth=0.5)

1099	At lag${=}0$ (contemporaneous), perplexity channels show \emph{higher} correlation than diversity channels ($r{=}0.990$ vs.\ $0.955$, $Z{=}-2.13$, $p{=}0.033$), reflecting the synchronous entropy-perplexity coupling.
1100	At lag${=}-1$ (canary leading by one generation), the pattern reverses decisively: diversity channels achieve $r{=}0.952$ vs.\ perplexity's $r{=}0.812$ ($Z{=}3.67$, $p{<}0.001$).
1101	This reversal explains the Granger $F$-statistic asymmetry: perplexity channels do not have weaker coupling overall, but their coupling is \emph{contemporaneous rather than lagged}.
1102	A Granger test measures whether \emph{past} values of $X$ predict \emph{current} $Y$ beyond $Y$'s own history; contemporaneous coupling produces zero improvement, hence $F \approx 0$.
1103	The Fisher Z analysis confirms that the canary$\to$perplexity non-detection is a correct statistical inference about temporal structure rather than a power limitation.

1221	
1222	\paragraph{Onset threshold sensitivity.}
1223	Table~\ref{tab:onset_threshold_sensitivity} examines how the cascade ordering result depends on the choice of onset threshold (5\%, 10\%, 15\%, 20\%).
1224	
1225	\begin{table}[t]
1226	\centering
1227	\small
1228	\caption{Cascade ordering $t_H \leq t_D$ validity as a function of onset threshold (Pythia-410M, 6 conditions, $\alpha{\geq}0.10$).}
1229	\label{tab:onset_threshold_sensitivity}
1230	\begin{tabular}{lccc}
1231	\toprule
1232	Threshold & $t_H \leq t_D$ & $t_H = t_D$ & $t_H > t_D$ \\
1233	\midrule
1234	5\% & 6/6 & 0/6 & 0/6 \\
1235	10\% & 6/6 & 0/6 & 0/6 \\
1236	15\% & 5/6 & 1/6 & 0/6 \\
1237	20\% & 4/6 & 2/6 & 0/6 \\
1238	\bottomrule
1239	\end{tabular}
1240	\end{table}
1241	
1242	At all thresholds, the ordering $t_H \leq t_D$ is never violated ($t_H > t_D$ in 0/24 cells).
1243	At the strictest threshold (5\%), entropy precedes diversity by exactly one generation in all 6 conditions.
1244	At relaxed thresholds (${\geq}15\%$), some conditions show simultaneous onset ($t_H = t_D$), because the larger threshold absorbs the diversity lag---consistent with the margin condition in Design Principle~\ref{prop:div_lag}.
1245	
1246	\paragraph{MAUVE cascade position.}
1247	MAUVE~\citep{pillutla2021mauve} onset occurs at generation~2--3, consistently after distinct-$n$ onset (gen-2), establishing a three-phase cascade: $\{H, \mathrm{ECE}\} \to \mathrm{Dist}\text{-}n \to \mathrm{MAUVE}$.
1248	MAUVE's later onset reflects its distributional-comparison nature: it measures the divergence between generated and reference text distributions, which requires substantial lexical collapse before the divergence exceeds the detection threshold.
1249	At $\alpha{=}1.0$, MAUVE floor saturation (${\sim}0.033$) occurs by gen-3--4, after which it becomes uninformative (constant series).
1250	
1251	\paragraph{Perplexity irregularity.}
1252	Perplexity onset is condition-dependent rather than cascade-ordered: at $\alpha{=}0.50$ (s42), PPL onset occurs at gen-4 (delayed relative to entropy gen-1 and diversity gen-2); at $\alpha{=}0.75$, PPL onset is gen-1 (contemporaneous with entropy).
1253	This irregularity is consistent with the first-differencing result: PPL is not temporally downstream of entropy in the Granger sense.
1254	Instead, PPL responds to a different aspect of collapse (cross-entropy on held-out data) that can accelerate or decelerate independently of the entropy-diversity cascade.
1255	
1256	
1257	\FloatBarrier
1258	\section{Cascade Ordering Theory: Derivations}
1259	\label{app:cascade_theory}
1260	
1261	This section provides the full derivations for Design Principle~\ref{thm:entropy_leads} and Design Principle~\ref{prop:div_lag} from \S\ref{sec:cascade}, plus supporting results.
1262	
1263	\begin{assumption}[Mode Amplification]\label{ass:mode_amp}
1264	The training map $\mathcal{T}$ redistributes probability mass from low-probability to high-probability tokens: there exists a threshold $\bar{p}$ such that $\mathcal{T}(p)_i > p_i \Rightarrow p_i \geq \bar{p}$ and $\mathcal{T}(p)_j < p_j \Rightarrow p_j \leq \bar{p}$.
1265	\end{assumption}
1266	
1267	This captures the standard finite-capacity approximation effect: MLE on finite samples from a peaked distribution amplifies modes and suppresses the tail~\citep{shumailov2024curse,dohmatob2024tale}.
1268	
1269	\begin{proposition}[Entropy is a first-order indicator]\label{prop:entropy_first}
1270	Let $p \in \Delta^{V-1}$ and $\delta = \alpha(\mathcal{T}(p) - p)$ be a mode-amplifying perturbation with $\sum_i \delta_i = 0$. Then $\Delta H = H(p+\delta) - H(p) = -\sum_i \delta_i \log p_i + O(\|\delta\|^2) < 0$.
1271	\end{proposition}
1272	
1273	\begin{proof}
1274	Taylor-expanding $H$ around $p$:
1275	$H(p+\delta) = H(p) - \sum_i \delta_i \log p_i - \sum_i \delta_i - \frac{1}{2}\sum_i \delta_i^2/p_i + O(\|\delta\|^3)$.
1276	Since $\sum_i \delta_i = 0$: $\Delta H = -\sum_i \delta_i \log p_i - \frac{1}{2}\sum_i \delta_i^2/p_i + O(\|\delta\|^3)$.
1277	Under mode amplification, tokens gaining mass ($\delta_i > 0$) have $p_i \geq \bar{p}$ (hence $\log p_i \geq \log \bar{p}$) and tokens losing mass ($\delta_j < 0$) have $p_j \leq \bar{p}$ (hence $\log p_j \leq \log \bar{p}$).
1278	Writing $m = \sum_{i:\delta_i > 0} \delta_i$: $-\sum_i \delta_i \log p_i = -m[\overline{\log p}^{+} - \overline{\log p}^{-}] < 0$, where $\overline{\log p}^{\pm}$ are the $|\delta|$-weighted means over gaining/losing tokens.
1279	The second-order Fisher information term $-\frac{1}{2}\sum_i \delta_i^2/p_i$ is always negative, reinforcing the decrease.
1280	\end{proof}
1281	
1282	\begin{proposition}[Diversity is a threshold indicator]\label{prop:div_threshold}
1283	Define the $\epsilon$-effective support $S_\epsilon(p) = |\{i \in [V]: p_i > \epsilon\}|$, where $\epsilon \approx c/N$ is the generation threshold for sample size $N$.
1284	Under the same perturbation $\delta$: $\Delta S_\epsilon = -|\{i: p_i > \epsilon \text{ and } p_i + \delta_i \leq \epsilon\}|$.
1285	This equals zero whenever $\|\delta\|_\infty < \gamma(p,\epsilon) := \min_{i: p_i > \epsilon}(p_i - \epsilon)$.
1286	\end{proposition}
1287	
1288	\begin{proof}
1289	Token $i$ exits the effective support iff $p_i > \epsilon$ and $p_i + \delta_i \leq \epsilon$, requiring $|\delta_i| \geq p_i - \epsilon$.

json.load(f)\n\nsv = d['main_result_6pair']\n\nmethod_order = ['naive_xcorr', 'diff_xcorr', 'threshold_onset', 'perm_granger', 'ty_granger']\nmethod_labels = ['Naive\\nXCorr', 'Diff.\\nXCorr', 'Threshold\\nOnset', 'Perm.\\nGranger', 'Toda-\\nYamamoto']\n\ncolors_pos = '#B2182B'\ncolors_neg = '#1B7837'\nhero_color = '#2166AC'\n\ndatasets = [\n    ('(a)  FPR contribution', sv['fpr_shapley'], 'increases FPR', 'reduces FPR'),\n    ('(b)  FNR contribution (6-pair)', sv['fnr_shapley'], 'increases FNR', 'reduces FNR'),\n    ('(c)  Combined (FPR + FNR)', sv['combined_shapley'], 'net harm', 'net benefit'),\n]\n\nx = np.arange(len(method_order))\n\ndef draw_panel(ax, title, vals_dict, pos_label, neg_label, show_xlabels=False):\n    vals = [vals_dict[m] for m in method_order]\n    bar_colors = []\n    for v, m in zip(vals, method_order):\n        if m == 'perm_granger':\n            bar_colors.append(hero_color)\n        elif v > 0:\n            bar_colors.append(colors_pos)\n        else:\n            bar_colors.append(colors_neg)\n\n    ax.bar(x, vals, 0.65, color=bar_colors, alpha=0.85,\n           edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.3)\n\n    for i, v in enumerate(vals):\n        offset = 0.012 if v >= 0 else -0.012\n        va = 'bottom' if v >= 0 else 'top'\n        fw = 'bold' if method_order[i] == 'perm_granger' else 'normal'\n        t = ax.text(i, v + offset, f'{v:+.3f}', ha='center', va=va,\n                    fontsize=6.5, fontweight=fw)\n        t.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=1.5, foreground='white')])\n\n    ax.axhline(0, color='black', linewidth=0.5)\n    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=9, fontweight='bold', loc='left', pad=4)\n    ax.set_ylabel('Shapley value', fontsize=8)\n    ax.yaxis.grid(True, alpha=0.15, linewidth=0.3)\n    ax.set_axisbelow(True)\n\n    ymin, ymax = ax.get_ylim()\n    ax.text(len(method_order) - 0.6, ymax * 0.85, pos_label, fontsize=6,\n            color=colors_pos, ha='right', va='top', fontstyle='italic', alpha=0.7)\n    ax.text(len(method_order) - 0.6, ymin * 0.85, neg_label, fontsize=6,\n            color=colors_neg, ha='right', va='bottom', fontstyle='italic', alpha=0.7)\n\n    if show_xlabels:\n        ax.set_xticks(x)\n        ax.set_xticklabels(method_labels, fontsize=7.5)\n    else:\n        ax.set_xticks(x)\n        ax.set_xticklabels([])\n\n# Group 1: panels (a) + (b)\nfig1, axes1 = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(3.4, 4.6), sharex=True)\ndraw_panel(axes1[0], *datasets[0])\ndraw_panel(axes1[1], *datasets[1], show_xlabels=True)\nfig1.tight_layout(h_pad=0.8)\nfor fmt in ['pdf', 'png']:\n    fig1.savefig(OUT / f'fig_shapley_contribution_top.{fmt}', dpi=300)\nplt.close(fig1)\n\n# Group 2: panel (c)\nfig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(1, 1, figsize=(3.4, 2.4))\ndraw_panel(ax2, *datasets[2], show_xlabels=True)\nfig2.tight_layout()\nfor fmt in ['pdf', 'png']:\n    fig2.savefig(OUT / f'fig_shapley_contribution_bottom.{fmt}', dpi=300)\nplt.close(fig2)\n\nprint('Saved {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — distrib_canary_collapse\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main Track** (D043, 用户指定)\\n- ARR cycle: June 2026 (deadline ~June 15, 待确认具体日期)\\n- 格式: ACL 系列模板 (acl.sty), 8 页正文 + unlimited appendix\\n- Note: ARR May cycle 已过；June cycle 对齐 EMNLP 2026 commitment deadline\\n\\n## Narrative Structure (D130: Discovery Paper Reframe)\\n\\n**主线**：Detection Phase Transition Discovery。我们发现 canary-downstream 因果检测能力在 α_c≈0.50 附近经历 steep onset (sigmoid R²=0.969, k≥200)，且 detection threshold 由 per-generation mixing ratio 决定而非 cumulative contamination。Multi-method 分析是发现这一现象的工具，不是贡献本身。\\n**四根支柱**：(1) Detection phase transition = C1 核心发现；(2) S×R 理论框架 + 5-regime 预测 = C2 机制解释；(3) Multi-method diagnostic toolkit (MMCT) = C3 方法贡献（降级为工具）；(4) Four-layer taxonomy + early cessation + data strategy = C4 实践贡献。\\n**Claim 呈现顺序**：C1 (phase transition discovery) 领衔 → C2 (theoretical framework) → C3 (multi-method toolkit) → C4 (practical applications)。\\n\\n## Core Claims (D130: 4 Contributions)\\n\\n> **Claim mapping (D130)**: D130 重组了贡献结构。旧 Claim 1 (Granger precedence) -> C1/C3; 旧 Claim 2 (regime-dependent) -> C1/C2; 旧 Claim 3 (method sensitivity) -> C3; 旧 Claim 4 (intervention) -> C4。下文 Key Narrative Constraints 中引用 \\\"Claim 1/2/3/4\\\" 仍指旧编号，按此映射解读。\\n\\n**C1: Detection Phase Transition (核心发现)** — Canary-downstream 因果检测能力在 alpha_c approx 0.476 附近经历 steep onset (at T=10; steepness T-dependent per C2 Power Theory)。Sigmoid fit R^2=0.969, steepness k>=200, transition width Δα median=0.022 (95% CI [0.008, 0.060]; D149)。Fisher exact p=3.45×10⁻¹¹ (0/64 vs 18/32, D148-corrected) provides model-free jump evidence。α_c location CI [0.43, 0.56] is 6× wider than Δα — position uncertainty, not transition gradualness。PermG 从 0/8 FDR-sig (alpha<=0.45; sub-threshold: fdr_p gradient 0.059–0.094, approaching detection threshold) 跳至 6/8 (alpha=0.50)，cross-seed validated (s42/s43/s44)。**核心 insight**: detection threshold 是 per-generation mixing ratio alpha，NOT cumulative contamination C(alpha,G)=1-(1-alpha)^G。alpha=0.45 cumulative=99.75% vs alpha=0.50=99.90% (0.15pp diff)，但 PermG 0/8 vs 6/8。形式化: Delta-h(g) proportional to alpha * d_TV(p_g^model, p^real)。Cross-architecture: PermG fwd >=6/8 in 10/14 combinations (Pythia-6.9B/1.4B/410M, GPT-2m, OPT-1.3B, Qwen3.5-0.8B, C4-Pythia-1.4B; exceptions: 1.4B+WikiText s42=2/8, SmolLM3=3/8)。**D145 domain×scale interaction (Reviewer #90 修正: seed-split, 非系统性失败)**: WikiText+1.4B α=1.0 是 seed-split（s42=2/8 vs s43=6/8），不是系统性检测失败。C4+1.4B=8/8 (s42)；WikiText+6.9B=7/8 恢复。Domain generalization confirmed; 1.4B WikiText 高方差反映 seed sensitivity 而非 scale limitation。当前 8 个 1.4B cells (T=10/15 × α=0.45/0.50/0.60/0.75) RUNNING，将进一步定位 α_c 是否 scale-dependent。Four-layer detection taxonomy: (1) effective_rank/dimensionality collapse alpha>=0.10, (2) distributional variance alpha>=0.25, (3) PermG causal coupling alpha>=0.50, (4) AC1 dynamical stability alpha>=0.50 (suggestive, T=11 insufficient)。Onset interval [0.45, 0.50] from 13-point alpha-sweep (alpha=0.25/0.30/0.35/0.40/0.45 all 0/8; alpha=0.25 borderline FDR p=0.053)。**CSD-Consistent Pattern (D138→D142 降级为 Discussion)**: Full-range canary CSD 趋势存在 (AR(1) τ=0.79-0.82, p<0.001), downstream 无趋势 (τ=0.03-0.30, p>0.19)。但 Reviewer WARNING: blind zone CSD 多重校正后仅 2/10 存活; \\\"cascade\\\" 解读缺 surrogate data test; onset jump 在 T=11 噪声内。**定位: Discussion suggestive evidence, 不进 Results**。措辞 \\\"CSD-consistent pattern\\\" 非 \\\"confirms CSD\\\"。Suggestive 4-stage temporal sequence: Structure (α=0.10) → Canary instability (α≥0.25) → Causal detection (α≥0.50) → Full cascade (α≥0.75)。T=20 重跑后可能升级。措辞约束 (D139 更新): \\\"detection phase transition\\\" / \\\"sharp onset\\\" / \\\"steep onset\\\" 允许; \\\"near-discontinuous onset\\\" 降级（PermG fdr_p gradient 0.059-0.094 显示信号 sub-threshold 非 absent; D143 retracted TY evidence）; \\\"first-order phase transition\\\" / \\\"phase transition\\\" (physical sense) 禁止。\\n\\n**C2: Theoretical Framework (机制解释)** — S(alpha) x R(alpha) 竞争模型: Detectability proportional to S(alpha) x R(alpha)。S = signal strength (monotonic increasing with alpha); R = temporal resolution (non-monotonic, drops at alpha approx 0.60 when relaxation time tau_relax(alpha) matches observation interval)。Predicts 4 regimes: (I) Blind zone alpha<0.45 — S too weak; (II) Onset alpha approx 0.45-0.50 — S crosses detection threshold; (III) Sync dip alpha approx 0.55-0.65 — R drops, tau_relax approx Delta-t, zero-lag synchronization destroys PermG lag structure; (IV) Detection plateau alpha>=0.70 — S dominates despite low R。Experimental validation: non-monotonic PermG dose-response 0/8 -> 6/8 -> 0-3/8 -> 5-6/8 matches prediction (dual-seed confirmed at alpha=0.60: s42=3/8, s43=0/8)。Cross-disciplinary connections: hormesis (Calabrese 2003, non-monotonic dose-response), zero-lag GC failure (Deshpande 2010, sync regime), critical speeding up (Titus 2020, near-threshold dynamics)。**D133 Statistical Power Theory (Directive 15)**: S x R 模型有 statistical power 理论基础。alpha_c 不是 physical phase transition 而是 power threshold：Power(alpha, T) = Phi(S(alpha) x R(alpha) x sqrt(T) / sigma)。当 Power 超过检测阈值时产生 \\\"detection phase transition\\\"。**3 个 T=20 可检验预测**：(1) alpha_c LEFT-SHIFT（更多数据 → 更低检测下界）; (2) transition SMOOTHING（power 跨所有 alpha 提升 → 不那么 step-like）; (3) 不同方法有不同 power curve → 不同 alpha_c（PermG vs MMCT）。**[RETRACTED D143] ~~TY=7/8 Smoking Gun~~**: Reviewer CRITICAL — s42 TY d_max=0→0/8 vs s43 d_max=2→7/8 完全由 d_max auto-selection 驱动 (df_resid=1 过拟合, chi2=5468)。TY=7/8 不可作为 Power Theory 验证。**Method-dependent α_c 理论仍成立**（spec curve 显示方法间 sensitivity 差异）但缺 T=11 直接实证——等 T=20 + 固定 d_max。PermG fdr_p gradient (s43 min=0.059, s42 min=0.091) 作为 sub-threshold evidence 仍有效（独立于 TY）。T=20 实验 RUNNING (t20_a050/a060/a075/a100 + t20_a045 待启) 验证预测(1)(2)(3)。Gu et al. (2025, NeurIPS Spotlight, arXiv:2505.18091) data mixing phase transitions 提供理论类比。\\n\\n**C3: Multi-Method Diagnostic Toolkit (降级为发现工具)** — MMCT (5 methods: naive_fwd + diff_fwd + threshold_onset + PermG + TY) 是发现 C1 的工具，非核心贡献本身。PermG null FPR=0/24 across 3 seeds (唯一 zero-FPR method)。K>=3 consensus 10/14 architecture-seed combinations。Me\\n\\n... \\\"near-discontinuous onset\\\" 降级（PermG fdr_p gradient 0.059-0.094 显示信号 sub-threshold 非 absent; D143 retracted TY evidence）; \\\"first-order phase transition\\\" / \\\"phase transition\\\" (physical sense) 禁止。\\n\\n**C2: Theoretical Framework (机制解释)** — S(alpha) x R(alpha) 竞争模型: Detectability proportional to S(alpha) x R(alpha)。S = signal strength (monotonic increasing with alpha); R = temporal resolution (non-monotonic, drops at alpha approx 0.60 when relaxation time tau_relax(alpha) matches observation interval)。Predicts 4 regimes: (I) Blind zone alpha<0.45 — S too weak; (II) Onset alpha approx 0.45-0.50 — S crosses detection threshold; (III) Sync dip alpha approx 0.55-0.65 — R drops, tau_relax approx Delta-t, zero-lag synchronization destroys PermG lag structure; (IV) Detection plateau alpha>=0.70 — S dominates despite low R。Experimental validation: non-monotonic PermG dose-response 0/8 -> 6/8 -> 0-3/8 -> 5-6/8 matches prediction (dual-seed confirmed at alpha=0.60: s42=3/8, s43=0/8)。Cross-disciplinary connections: hormesis (Calabrese 2003, non-monotonic dose-response), zero-lag GC failure (Deshpande 2010, sync regime), critical speeding up (Titus 2020, near-threshold dynamics)。**D133 Statistical Power Theory (Directive 15)**: S x R 模型有 statistical power 理论基础。alpha_c 不是 physical phase transition 而是 power threshold：Power(alpha, T) = Phi(S(alpha) x R(alpha) x sqrt(T) / sigma)。当 Power 超过检测阈值时产生 \\\"detection phase transition\\\"。**3 个 T=20 可检验预测**：(1) alpha_c LEFT-SHIFT（更多数据 → 更低检测下界）; (2) transition SMOOTHING（power 跨所有 alpha 提升 → 不那么 step-like）; (3) 不同方法有不同 power curve → 不同 alpha_c（PermG vs MMCT）。**[RETRACTED D143] ~~TY=7/8 Smoking Gun~~**: Reviewer CRITICAL — s42 TY d_max=0→0/8 vs s43 d_max=2→7/8 完全由 d_max auto-selection 驱动 (df_resid=1 过拟合, chi2=5468)。TY=7/8 不可作为 Power Theory 验证。**Method-dependent α_c 理论仍成立**（spec curve 显示方法间 sensitivity 差异）但缺 T=11 直接实证——等 T=20 + 固定 d_max。PermG fdr_p gradient (s43 min=0.059, s42 min=0.091) 作为 sub-threshold evidence 仍有效（独立于 TY）。T=20 实验 RUNNING (t20_a050/a060/a075/a100 + t20_a045 待启) 验证预测(1)(2)(3)。Gu et al. (2025, NeurIPS Spotlight, arXiv:2505.18091) data mixing phase transitions 提供理论类比。\\n\\n**C3: Multi-Method Diagnostic Toolkit (降级为发现工具)** — MMCT (5 methods: naive_fwd + diff_fwd + threshold_onset + PermG + TY) 是发现 C1 的工具，非核心贡献本身。PermG null FPR=0/24 across 3 seeds (唯一 zero-FPR method)。K>=3 consensus 10/14 architecture-seed combinations。Me\\n\\n... {
  "project": "distrib_canary_collapse",
  "text": "**请独立审查 Round 15 审稿报告及以下初步 Action Matrix。** 重点关注：(1) 我对每条的 evidence vs writing 判断是否正确；(2) 方案可行性；(3) 是否有遗漏的问题。\n\n## Round 15 审稿报告核心问题\n\n**Overall: 3.0 (Findings)**, R1=3.5, R2=3.0。\n\n## 逐条 Action Matrix\n\n---\n\n### MF1: 跨架构泛化仅在 α=1.0 [共识, Evidence 问题]\n**问题**: Phase transition boundary (α≈0.45) 没有跨架构验证。标题暗示普适性但实际仅基于 Pythia-410M。\n**复发**: 非复发。R1 Round1 \"实验规模过窄\" 已通过 15 model-seed combos resolved，但那些都在 α=1.0。R15 从新角度——boundary 位置泛化性——重新提出。\n**判断**: Evidence 问题。不能只改写作。\n**方案 A（补实验）**: 在 Pythia-1.4B 上做 α∈{0.45, 0.50, 0.75} 的 PermG 验证。当前 8 个 1.4B cells RUNNING，若含 α-sweep 可直接用。预估 GPU: ~8h per α per seed。\n**方案 B（降级 claim）**: 标题改为 scope-qualified (如 \"…on Pythia-410M\")；Abstract/Conclusion 加 \"at α=1.0\" qualifier for cross-arch claims。Table 1 caption 明确标注 \"α=1.0 only\"。\n**推荐**: A+B 并行。即使补了实验也应加 scope qualifier。\n\n---\n\n### MF2: Power-law boundary 拟合欠定 [共识, Evidence 问题]\n**问题**: 3 自由参数拟合 3-4 个数据点 = 插值非回归。γ CI [0.01, 2.10] 跨两个数量级。\n**复发**: 非复发。新问题。\n**判断**: Evidence 问题。但短期内无法增加足够 α 点（每个需完整 pipeline）。\n**方案 A（补实验）**: 增加 α∈{0.35, 0.40, 0.55, 0.65, 0.70} 的实验。但每个 α 需 ~3h GPU × 10 generations。总计 ~15h。\n**方案 B（降级 claim）**: 将 power-law boundary 从 quantitative claim 降为 \"observational trend\"。正文承认 CI 过宽，删除精确 γ 值报告，保留方向性结论 \"detection improves with longer observation\"。\n**方案 C（改统计方法）**: 用 profile likelihood 报告 γ 的置信区间而非 point estimate。已在 Appendix 有 profile likelihood，正文引用即可。\n**推荐**: B+C。精确 γ 值没有统计意义，但方向性结论有。\n\n---\n\n### MF3: PermG FPR 6.7% CI 过宽 [共识, Evidence + Writing]\n**问题**: 60 null pairs 的 exact binomial 95% CI [1.8%, 16.2%]。CI 上界与 DiffXCorr 14.8% 重叠。\n**复发**: R1-N2 \"FPR 标定仅基于单 seed\" 已 resolved（扩到 10 seeds）。R15 新角度：即使 10 seeds，60 pairs 的 CI 仍太宽。\n**判断**: Writing 问题为主（正文需报告 CI），部分 Evidence（增加 null pairs 有限空间）。\n**方案**: \n1. §4.1 和 Conclusion 中 \"6.7%\" 后附 CI \"[1.8%, 16.2%]\"\n2. Conclusion 改措辞：从 \"combining the lowest FPR 6.7%\" 改为 \"combining the lowest point-estimate FPR (6.7%, CI [1.8%, 16.2%])\"\n3. 讨论 CI 重叠：承认 PermG vs DiffXCorr FPR 差异在统计上不显著，但 point estimate ranking 有信息价值\n**推荐**: 纯写作修复即可。\n\n---\n\n### MF4: \"Detectability landscape\" 概念新颖性 [分歧, Framing 问题]\n**问题**: R2 认为 landscape = dose-response rebranding。R1 反驳 multi-method + non-monotonic 超越 dose-response，但同意术语 inflation。\n**复发**: R2-N1 Round1 \"方法论迁移\" 已 resolved。R15 从术语角度重新提出。\n**判断**: Writing/Framing 问题。\n**方案**:\n1. 标题保留 \"Detectability Landscape\" 但在 §1 intro 明确定义为 \"the joint space of contamination level, observation length, and detection method\"——不仅仅是 dose-response\n2. 正文降低 novelty claim 强度：从 \"we characterize the landscape\" 改为 \"we map the multi-method detection sensitivity profile\"\n3. 保留 non-monotonic anomaly 和 multi-method comparison 作为 landscape 概念超越 dose-response 的证据\n**推荐**: 适度降低 claim 但保留术语。\n\n---\n\n### MF5: Method sensitivity 定位过高 [分歧→共识, Framing 问题]\n**问题**: \"不同统计检验有不同 power/FPR\" 是常识。14x FPR 差异虽大但定位为 Contribution 1 偏高。\n**复发**: 非复发。但与 paper_framing.md D130 reframe 相关（已将 detection phase transition 作为 C1）。\n**判断**: Writing 问题。当前 framing 已经将 phase transition 作为 C1，method sensitivity 不在 headline。需验证当前论文正文是否仍把 method sensitivity 放在 Contribution 1 位置。\n**方案**: 检查 §1 contribution list 排序，确保 (1) detection phase transition, (2) theoretical framework, (3) multi-method toolkit, (4) practical applications。如果当前已是此顺序则只需微调措辞。\n\n---\n\n### SF1: 3 seeds 不足 [共识, Evidence 问题]\n**判断**: Evidence 问题但短期无法解决（每 seed 需完整 pipeline）。\n**方案**: Limitations 中诚实报告 seed 数量限制及其对 variance 估计的影响。已在 Limitations 有相关内容。\n**推荐**: 写作补强 limitation acknowledgment。\n\n---\n\n### SF2: MMCT 增量价值有限 [共识, Framing 问题]\n**问题**: PermG 已是最优单方法时 MMCT 增量价值不明。\n**复发**: 非复发，但 paper_framing 已将 MMCT 降级为 \"发现工具\"。\n**判断**: Writing 问题。需进一步降级。\n**方案**: \n1. Limitations 增加一句 MMCT 的 honest assessment：\"MMCT provides marginal improvement over standalone PermG in the current experimental setting\"\n2. §3 和 Discussion 中弱化 MMCT 的方法论 claim\n**推荐**: 写作调整。\n\n---\n\n### SF3: Intervention 正文篇幅过多 [共识, Writing 问题]\n**问题**: §4.6 仅在 α=1.0 验证但占 ~0.5 页。\n**判断**: Writing 问题。\n**方案**: 将 §4.6 压缩到 1-2 段（保留核心结论：predictive trigger 比 reactive 早 1 generation，peak PPL 降 64%），细节移入附录。\n**推荐**: 写作压缩。\n\n---\n\n### SF4: Entropy artifact 应在主文讨论 [共识, Evidence + Writing]\n**问题**: Entropy 是连续量、distinct-n 是离散计数——对 Design Principle 1 的替代解释。应从 Appendix J 提升到 §5 Discussion。审稿人还要求 discretized entropy test。\n**复发**: F8-N3 \"Appendix 关键验证内容应纳入正文\" 在 R15 再次出现（相同方向但新角度）。[复发 × 2 轮] 之前的 writing 修复（增加 appendix 引用）显然不够——R15 要求提升到 Discussion。\n**判断**: Evidence + Writing。Discretized entropy test 是新实验。提升到 Discussion 是写作。\n**方案 A（写作）**: §5 Discussion 增加 1 段讨论 entropy 测量分辨率 vs 因果 precedence 的 concern，引用 Appendix J 的分析。\n**方案 B（补实验）**: 对 entropy 做 binning（与 distinct-n 相同分辨率）后重新跑 Granger precedence test。预估 GPU: ~2h（仅分析，无需重新训练）。\n**推荐**: A+B 并行。写作是必须的，discretized test 成本很低且能直接回应审稿人。\n\n---\n\n### SF5: 缺 Silberzahn (2018) [共识, Writing 问题]\n**方案**: §2 Related Work 或 §3.3 添加 Silberzahn et al. (2018) 引用，1-2 句讨论 multi-analyst framework 与本文 multi-method comparison 的关系。\n**推荐**: 写作修复。\n\n---\n\n### SF6: 核心内容过度依赖附录 [部分共识, Writing 问题]  \n**方案**: §3.1 Design Principle 增加 1-2 句 intuitive justification（不需要完整推导，只需直觉解释 why entropy leads diversity）。\n**推荐**: 写作修复。\n\n---\n\n### 其他较低优先级：\n- **Table 2 K≥4/K≥5 冗余**: 合并为脚注或删除\n- **Figure 1 低值区颜色**: 考虑换色板\n- **Design Principle 正文缺中间推导**: 同 SF6\n- **PermG direction checking 正文不足**: §3.3 增加 1-2 句\n- **缺 Kirchenbauer (2023)**: §2 Related Work 添加简要讨论\n\n---\n\n## 综合判断\n\n**Evidence 问题**（不能只改写作）: MF1, MF2, SF1, SF4(discretized test)\n**Writing/Framing 问题**: MF3, MF4, MF5, SF2, SF3, SF4(Discussion 段), SF5, SF6, 其他\n\n**关键决策点**: \n1. MF1 是否需要补跨架构 α-sweep 实验？当前 1.4B 实验 RUNNING 可能已覆盖部分 α 值。需确认。\n2. MF2 的 power-law claim 降级方案是否足够？还是必须补实验？\n3. SF4 的 discretized entropy test 是否值得做？（我认为值得，成本低且直接回应审稿人）\n\n请 Reviewer 独立评估以上方案的合理性，特别是：\n- 哪些 Evidence 问题可以通过降级 claim 充分回应？\n- 哪些必须补实验？\n- 有没有我遗漏的问题？"
}
```
